- **2026-08-12** [fly 231 · HOTFIX B4.1a] **Panel pin/overlap defect — ✅ SHIPPED** — code `4be2f55`; readback exact. **The defect (live on fly 230, founder screenshot):** the cards section scrolled OVER the pinned black panel while the dots were still showing. **Root cause, two layers:** (H2, primary) the pinned section's parent was `flex flex-col` — ScrollTrigger's pin-spacer became a FLEX ITEM and flex sizing squished it, so the `+=350%` reservation never held; (H1-adjacent, secondary) `useFadeIn` permanently retained `transform: translateY(0)` on the cards section — a stacking context that painted the later-DOM cards OVER the `position:fixed` panel once the spacer failure let them meet. **Fix:** wrapper flex removed (children are stacked block sections; the spacer now reserves in normal block flow) + the fade hook clears all inline styles on `transitionend`. **Verified with a real browser scroll-through (dev + Chrome):** pin-spacer 3823px, `position:fixed` held across the range, width scrubs 70→95vw, segments advance, reverse clean, **zero overlap at 13 measured scroll positions**; static path verified structurally (it is the SSR default; untouched by the fix — Gate 5 rows 4–5 stay the founder's check). **LESSON: entry-animation transforms and ScrollTrigger pins don't mix — exclude pinned sections from generic entry animations, clear transform residue on completion, and never give a pinned section a flex parent.**

- **2026-08-10** [fly 218] **`/api/share/create` citation contract — typed + bounded, `local` TOMBSTONED AT WRITE — ✅ SHIPPED** — code `d85e85e`, docs `<this commit>`. **Deployed 2026-08-10, fly version 218, nrt, image `deployment-01KZN4R4CZAS7AR8841YQ4Y3XH`; `.\deploy.ps1` clean; machines 683d447c2e5428 started + 2879720c66d478 stopped — both v218, the known benign auto-start false-negative.**
  > **✅ STEP 0 — BUILD CONFIRMED BY READBACK** (the fly-217 capability, used for the first time on a real ship): `/health` `revision` = **`d85e85e388528e06ca6fbfa7cb6c63d6033ce9da`** = the pushed SHA, exact match.
  > **What:** `ShareCreateRequest.citations` was a bare `list` — no item schema, no bounds — stored verbatim onto a **public, SEO-indexed** page. Now `list[CitationIn]`, **max 50**, every string bounded (`url` ≤2048, `snippet`/`abstract`/`text` ≤8000, `source_type` ≤64). **Field set DERIVED, not invented:** the 10 fields of `schemas.Citation` (what the SSE emits and `ShareModal` forwards back) **+ `abstract`/`text`**, which the renderer reads as snippet fallbacks; `id`/`source_id` Optional because stored `explore_page` rows are observed without them.
  > **POSTURE — LENIENT, deliberately NOT reject-on-unknown.** `source_type` and `credibility` are bounded **strings, not enums**: rejecting unknowns would break the open **#7** population (stale clients replaying pre-c1 answers carrying `local`) and would 422 on **`clinical-trial`/`review`**, valid `CredibilityLevel` members the renderer has no pill for. **The hole closed is the UNBOUNDED payload, not unknown values.** Only **structural** violations are rejected → normal 422.
  > **Rule 19 — ONE transform, two callers.** `share_renderer.tombstone_citation` is now the single definition, called by `_augment_citations` (render) **and** the store site (write). Data-level only — it bakes in **no render chrome**, because the label is locale-dependent ("Source withdrawn" / 「來源已撤回」) and persisting one would freeze a locale into the row. **Both layers stay:** write side stops any NEW untombstoned row; **render side still covers the 8 pre-c1 stubs deliberately retained in prod** (deleting them renumbers the prose). Removing either reopens a population.
  > **🔴 A COLLISION FOUND AND FIXED IN THE SAME CHANGE:** 422 now has **two senders**. The PHI block sends `type='share_sensitive_blocked'`; FastAPI validation sends `detail` and no `type`. Undiscriminated, a malformed payload would have rendered the **"sensitive content"** copy — telling the user something **false about their own data**. `ShareModal` now discriminates and falls back to the generic error.
  > **Gates: pytest 173 passed** (was 161 — **6 new write-path cases**: local stored tombstoned · pubmed/tfda/dailymed byte-identical · unknown `source_type` accepted untouched · 5 structural payloads rejected · **write-vs-render parity pin** · a guard that the model declares every field the renderer reads, since Pydantic would otherwise drop it silently). **`npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0.** **NO human-eye render gate required and none run** — rendering logic is unchanged and the parity test pins it; the change is write-path plus a one-function refactor.
  > ❌ ~~⏳ **FOUNDER UI SPOT-CHECK REQUESTED**…~~ → ✅ **FOUNDER UI SPOT-CHECK: PASS (2026-08-10, `/q/80DFqlk4jGQ` on fly 218).** **5 citations — 3 DailyMed + 2 PubMed** — all rendering **source labels**, **credibility pills** (Official / Peer Reviewed) and **working View-source deep links**. **No collateral tombstoning of real sources. No FDA chip.** **This closes the fly-218 verification loop:** test (b) held at the data layer, and the founder confirmed it end to end.

Two independent systems, **no coupling** (verified: nothing in the citation path reads a section marker). Three consecutive DailyMed cards are the "uniform blue". ⚠️ **One detail of the working reading corrected rather than smoothed over: it is the source-LABEL TEXT, not a left border** — an exhaustive search found only **four** `border-left` rules in the whole template tree, none on a citation card. `/q/` and `/explore` are **byte-identical** here. Origin `a5da1c5`, which states the intent (`_SOURCE_TYPE_CONFIG` *"ported exactly from"* the app's CitationPanel) — **deliberate parity, not drift**. **Instrument-blind #15 filed, self-attributed:** one of two authorized prod queries was spent measuring the wrong component.

- **2026-07-29** [fly 214] **Citation "View source" deep-link fix — FDA(local) chips no longer destroy the user's answer — PROD HUMAN-EYE GATE PASS, every source type verified** — **DEPLOYED 2026-07-29 (fly version 214, nrt, image `deployment-01KYNRXCYAFP2VWMH14HCFJ731`; /health 200 version 2.2.0; BOTH machines `2879720c66d478` + `683d447c2e5428` on 214, both `started` — verified by hand-parsing the `│` rows, NOT from deploy.ps1's known-[P3] parser claim).** **FRONTEND-ONLY — ONE file changed since fly 213 (`components/CitationPanel.tsx`); zero `api/`, zero `.py`, `fly.toml` untouched, no flags, no secrets. §2.7 NOT required** (no retrieval / generation / citation-*selection* change); a human-eye gate WAS, and it passed. **The bug:** the local drug corpus ships `url: ""` on all 690 documents (`scripts/build_drug_vectordb.py` hard-codes it at 5 sites), so the References panel rendered `<a href="">` — which navigates to the CURRENT page and remounts this static-export SPA at its empty state, **destroying the user's answer and references**. **NOT a regression:** `CitationPanel.tsx` and the whole citation-producer path are unchanged since fly 206 — **the FDA-chip deep-link has NEVER worked**; it became visible because fly 209/211 raised how often official-label sections reach the cited pool. **A gate-coverage gap, not a coding slip** — the fly-206 gate verified the **DailyMed** deep-link only, and the FDA chip went unclicked through three subsequent gates. **The fix = BRANCH (3), suppression, NOT a synthesized URL:** the corpus has **no stable per-document identifier** (`full_label.url` = `https://labels.fda.gov/` on 190/190; `source_id` = `FDA:<brand name>`; no `spl_set_id`/`application_number` anywhere), so a URL built from a drug name would be a **search, not the source document** — a false provenance claim of the same family as the v199/v201 "FDA Label Analysis" honesty [P1]. New exported pure helper **`isUsableSourceUrl()`** (requires an absolute `http(s)` URL; rejects `''`/whitespace/`null`, relative, scheme-less, `javascript:`, `data:`) now gates the anchor. **No new user-visible string → Rule 16 not triggered, no 16-language work.** **Corpus NOT rebuilt** — `data/drug_vectordb/index.json` is byte-identical, so the next baton's retrieval baseline is unaffected by construction. **TESTS — `tests/citation_url_guard.mjs`, both layers NEGATIVE-CONTROLLED:** DATA (every DailyMed + TFDA doc has a usable `https://` URL; `local` is a **documented exemption that fails loudly if local ever GAINS urls**, so a stale exemption cannot pass silently) — injected an empty-url DailyMed doc → **failed as required**, restored; RENDER (helper semantics + the component must actually **gate** the anchor, not merely export the helper) — reverted the gate to `{true && (` → **failed as required**, restored. `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0. **PROD VERIFY:** the fix is compiled into the deployed chunk `research-757f6f067e7cc77a.js` and gating the anchor; **b1 regression check clean on the new chunk** (all SG/MY/TW authority URLs, both timezones, `localeDebug`, TW `健保給付` override, `cat_labels` ×2, **0** NHI in i18n keys); `/research` + `/settings` 200; log-grep clean (0 ERROR/CRITICAL/Traceback/RERANK_SKIP/SOURCE_WEIGHT_INERT). ⚠️ **Anonymous Research smoke could NOT be run on prod** — `POST /api/research` returns **403 `{"detail":"Missing token"}`** with and without an `Origin` header; the streaming/reference path is covered by the founder's signed-in gate instead. **Lever state (BY NAME, UNCHANGED — frontend-only ship touched no flags):** `RETRIEVAL_REFUSAL_SHADOW`=ON · `SOURCE_WEIGHT_SHADOW`=ON · `SOURCE_WEIGHT_ACTIVE`=ON (3 ON share digest `d8c5ac2e11c8e492`, Deployed) · `DIRECTION_CHECK_SHADOW`=OFF · `QUESTION_NEUTRALIZATION_SHADOW`=OFF (absent = unset = code default) · **locale-hint=ON via `fly.toml [build.args]`** (build-arg, never in `fly secrets list`); **no staged secrets.** **PROD HUMAN-EYE GATE: PASS (founder, signed-in, every source type).** Row 1 = the founder's original report *"What are the common side effects of Metformin?"* → **the FDA(local) chip renders NO "View source" — ✅ PASS BY DESIGN, NOT A DEFECT** (a missing link is honest; the link that existed destroyed the answer; a fabricated link would be worse than both); DailyMed + PubMed links open in a new tab with answer + references intact. Row 2 warfarin+aspirin → PubMed + DailyMed both resolve. **TFDA resolved on retry** (the checklist's phrasing was too weak — see finding 2). **Row 5: no LINKED "FDA" chip appeared in any query**, giving the openFDA structural-inertness conclusion observational support on top of code inspection. **⚠️ SIX FINDINGS RECORDED, NOTHING FIXED — full record [`docs/citation_gate_findings_20260729.md`](docs/citation_gate_findings_20260729.md):** **(1) [the big one, new [P1] proposed] the 690-doc `local` corpus is 100% EMPTY STUBS** — payload (content minus the builder's literal template scaffolding) is **0 on 190 docs (27.5%), ≤20 chars on ALL 690, max 5, median 1**; `pharmacology` yields **0** documents; **0/690** can ever show "Show more". **Root cause proven in code:** `collect_drug_data.py`'s `_extract_*` methods were written for the **raw openFDA list shape** but receive `FDADrugLabel.to_dict()`, a flat dict of **strings** — so `[0]` returns the **first character** (`'6'`, `'4'`, `'7'`, `'W'`… verified 190/190 exact match) and the raw-only key names (`indications_and_usage`, `dosage_and_administration`, `clinical_pharmacology`, `openfda`) fall through to `""`. **≈3.58M chars of real FDA text sit UNREAD in `full_label`.** Embeddings are cleanly separated **by drug name + section label and nothing else** — which is exactly why they reach `top_k` and then contribute nothing, while Tier-2-boosted **×1.5 (composite ×2.25) above Tier-4 PubMed studies**. **The deprecation question is now three-way (deprecate / repair / keep) and the ratified `local = Tier 2` premise ("local IS cached FDA labeling") is REFUTED** — re-opened for the founder, not reversed. **(2)** the TFDA row's failure is a **live prod confirmation of TECH_DEBT [P3] phrasing-sensitivity** (`冠脂妥適應症查詢` → no TFDA; **`冠脂妥台灣核准的適應症是什麼` → TFDA 核准適應症 1**) — canonical gate query now named in the `[ops]` SOP; **TFDA deep-link BROWSER-CONFIRMED**, closing the last Task-1c gap, and **my Task-1c wording was CORRECTED — `im_detail_pdf` is a per-licence DETAIL PAGE, not a direct PDF**; plus a recorded **"TFDA" naming collision** (citation chip vs locale-panel pointer) with three disambiguation options proposed, none applied. **(3) [ESCALATED]** the b1 locale panel **contradicts itself on one screen** — 「Vela 未整合上述機關資料」 rendered unconditionally (`LocaleHintPanel.tsx:165`, 16 locales) on a page whose only source IS the TFDA corpus → the concrete user-visible instance of BACKLOG **(d) pointer→grounding graduation**, priority-raise proposed; **class = a string that was TRUE when written and became FALSE when the data shipped**, the sibling of b1's "NHI reimbursement" leak (**proposed Rule-19 amendment — CLAUDE.md NOT edited**). **(4)** panel fired 4/5; the multi-category zh case proves the classifier is not collapsing to Dosing, so any over-firing concern is **English-list-specific** → open measurement item [P3], keyword list untouched. **(5)** FDA(local) cards look broken to users — recorded with Rule 16 + the finding that the **existing** tooltip already over-claims, **plus a newly-found wrong-agency tooltip: the TFDA chip also uses `officialTip` ("From official FDA drug labeling data")**; **sequenced behind the deprecation decision** (if the corpus goes, the 16-lang work is wasted). **(6) [has a clock on it]** the TFDA corpus **cites licences that expire** — 17,881 cited licences joined to the pinned snapshot: **71 (0.4%) already expired; 12.0% within 12 months; 36.3% within 24**; **有效日期 was DROPPED at build → no runtime staleness check is possible**; and the **deep-link anchor rots FASTER than the document** (**40 docs already link to an expired licence, 476 within 6 months; 1,223 (11.2%) chose a representative expiring sooner than one of its own siblings** — the gate's own citation anchors on the earliest of 12, 2027-01-18 vs a sibling valid to 2031-07-18). Monthly re-pull is **due now** per the snapshot manifest; cheapest independent win = prefer the latest-expiring representative (one sort key). **Commits:** `b19d3a8` (Phase-1 correction + openFDA [P2] with its hard ordering constraint) + `<docs commit>` (this ledger + the six findings + TECH_DEBT/BACKLOG). **📌 .md changes FOUNDER-PENDING for PK sync.**
